Keywords: Genjutsu, Visual, Unaware

On a failed save, they gain 2 ranks of fear for the

FORGET ME NOT
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: B-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Minute
Range: Touch
Duration: Special
Components: HS, CM, NT (Poison Kit or Poisonous Flower)
Cost: 11 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Inhale, Unaware
Description: You infuse chakra into a poisonous flower
that you are holding. When you release the flower, all
creatures except yourself within 5 feet of the flower are hit
with a powerfully potent, yet odorless plume of poisonous
pollen laced with your chakra. Creatures within range must
make a Constitution and Intelligence saving throw, in that
order.
On a Failed Constitution saving throw, the affected
creature becomes poisoned for 1 hour (This cannot become
envenomed) and a creature poisoned by this jutsu makes
the next saving throw at disadvantage.
On a failed Intelligence saving throw, they lose all
memory of the past 24 hours, for the next hour as it
becomes a haze. This memory is still there, but clouded
and hazy. If they attempt to remember, they must make
an Intelligence save throw at disadvantage.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above B-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3. When
you cast this jutsu at A-Rank, on a failed save, their
memory is permanently clouded over.

Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: B-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Self (10-foot radius)
Duration: Permanent
Components: HS, CM, CS
Cost: 14 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Visual, Unaware

target must currently be incapacitated or willing.
Over the course of a minute, you tattoo a seal on a
target creatures’ tongue. You select 1 topic, which you
wish for the target creature to be unable to speak of.
Once this jutsu is cast the target creature becomes
unable to speak, write, or willing reveal information on
or about the topic you chose. A creature can attempt to
undo the effects of this jutsu by making a Ninshou skill
check against your Ninjutsu Save DC + 5. Each attempt
takes 1 hour. On a successful check the target creature
becomes able to speak on the topic freely again.

MAJOR IMAGE
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: B-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 120 Feet
Duration: Until Dispelled
Components: HS, CM, CS
Cost: 12 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Auditory, Visual, Tactile
Description: You create the image of an object, a
creature, or some other visible phenomenon that is no
larger than a 20-foot cube. The image appears at a spot
that you can see within range and lasts for the duration.
It seems completely real, including sounds, smells, and
temperature appropriate to the thing depicted. You can’t
create sufficient heat or cold to cause damage, a sound
loud enough to deal damage or deafen a creature, or a
smell that might sicken a creature.
As long as you are within range of the illusion, you can
use your action to cause the image to move to any other
spot within range. As the image changes location, you
can alter its appearance so that its movements appear
natural for the image. For example, if you create an
image of a creature and move it, you can alter the image
so that it appears to be walking. Similarly, you can cause
the illusion to make different sounds at different times,
even making it carry on a conversation, for example.
Physical interaction with the image reveals it to be a
genjutsu, because things can pass through it. A creature
that uses its action to examine the image can determine
that it is an illusion with a successful Intelligence
(Investigation) or Wisdom (Illusion) check against your
genjutsu save DC. If a creature discerns the illusion for
what it is, the creature can see through the image, and
its other sensory qualities become faint to the creature.

Classification: Genjutsu

Range: Self (15-foot Radius)
Duration: Instant
Components: HS, CM
Cost: 14 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu
Description: You take the Release technique and apply it

chakra to dispel genjutsu. You must first be aware that
the targets in the area are under the effects of a genjutsu.
As part of the casting of this jutsu, make a Constitution
(Chakra Control) check at advantage against the saving
throw DC of the genjutsu the targets are being affected

MEMORY DOMINATION
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: B-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Minute
Range: Touch
Duration: Until Restored
Components: HS, CM, CS
Cost: 14 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Fuinjutsu
Description: You attempt to reshape another creature’s
memories. This jutsu can only be cast on a willing or
unconscious creature. The creature must make an
Intelligence saving throw. On a failed save, you are able
to affect the target’s memory of any event that it
experienced within the last 12 Months and that lasted no
more than 1 Day.
You can Temporarily overwrite all memory of the
event, allow the target to recall the event with perfect
clarity and exacting detail, change its memory of the
details of the event, or create a memory of some other
event. Its mind fills in any gaps in the details of your
description. If the jutsu ends before you have finished
describing the modified memories, the creature’s
memory isn’t altered.
The modified memories take hold when the jutsu
ends. A modified memory doesn’t necessarily affect how
a creature behaves, particularly if the memory
contradicts the creature’s natural inclinations,
allegiance, or beliefs. An illogical modified memory is
dismissed, perhaps as a bad dream. The DM might deem
a modified memory too nonsensical to affect a creature
in a significant manner.
A Genjutsu Break or Greater Restorative Jutsu cast on
the target, specifically intended to restore the creature’s
true memory will restore the memory.

immediately.
DEVILS AMBITION
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: A-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 Feet
Duration: 1 Minute
Components: HS, CM, NT (Poison Kit)
Cost: 18 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Inhale
Description: You take a venomous concoction of
different steroids and performance enhancers and
release it into the air, manipulating it to affect and
enhance up to 2 willing creatures you can see within
range.
Affected creatures gain the following benefits for the
duration;
• Whenever they make an D20 roll that lets you add your
proficiency bonus, you can treat a d20 roll of 7 or
lower as a 8.
• They gain a +2 bonus to all attack rolls.
• They gain resistance against all Genjutsu with the
Auditory Keyword.
• They gain an additional attack that they can only use
once per casting.
At the conclusion of this jutsu, affected creatures
become poisoned for 10 minutes, which cannot be
removed by any means and gain 1 rank of exhaustion.

DOMINATE MAN
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: A-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 Feet
Duration: Concentration, up to 1 Hour
Components: HS, CM
Cost: 20 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Auditory
Description: You attempt to beguile a humanoid that you
can see within range. It must make on a Wisdom saving
throw or be charmed by you for the duration. If you or
creatures that are friendly to you are fighting it, it has
advantage on the saving throw.
While the target is charmed, you have a telepathic link
with it as long as the two of you are within 1 mile of each
other. You can use this telepathic link to issue
commands to the creature while you are conscious (no
action required), which it does its best to obey. You can
specify a simple and general course of action, such as
"Attack that creature", "Run over there", or "Fetch that
object". If the creature completes the order and doesn’t
receive further direction from you, it defends and
preserves itself to the best of its ability.
You can use your action to take total and precise
control of the target. Until the end of your next turn, the
creature takes only the actions you choose, and doesn’t
do anything that you don’t allow it to do. During this
time, you can also cause the creature to use a reaction,
but this requires you to use your own reaction as well.
Each time the target takes damage, it makes a new
Wisdom saving throw against the spell. If the saving
throw succeeds, the genjutsu ends.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above A-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 5. If this
jutsu is cast at S-Rank or Higher, you may now target
any creature instead of just Humanoid creatures and the
creature instead only makes additional saves at the end
of each of their turns.
GEAS
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: A-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 Feet
Duration: 30 Days
Components: HS, CM
Cost: 20 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Auditory, Tactile
Description: You place a genjutsu based command on a
creature that you can see within range, forcing it to carry
out some service or refrain from some action or course of
activity as you decide. If the creature can understand you, it
must make on a Wisdom saving throw or become charmed
by you for the duration.
While the creature is charmed by you, it takes 9d10 psychic
damage each time it acts in a manner directly counter to
your instructions, but no more than once each day. A
creature that can’t understand you is unaffected by the
genjutsu. You can issue any command you choose, short of
an activity that would result in certain death. Should you
issue a suicidal command, the genjutsu ends. You can end
the spell early as a free action.

MIRAGE
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: A-Rank
Casting Time: 10 minutes
Range: 1 Mile
Duration: 10 Days
Components: HS, CM, CS
Cost: 19 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Auditory, Tactile, Inhale, Visual
Description: You make terrain in an area up to 1 square
mile look, sound, smell and even feel like some other
sort of terrain. The terrain's general shape remains the
same, however, open fields or a road could be made to
resemble a swamp, hill, crevasse, or some other difficult
or impassable terrain. You can also alter the appearance
of structures or add them where none are present.
This does not affect creatures in the area. Creatures
with true sight can see through the illusion to the
terrain’s true form however, all other elements of the
illusion remain, so while the creature is aware of the
illusion’s presence, the creature can still physically
interact with the illusion.

Poison trees unable to leave the 30ft sphere. For the

Genjutsu take 5d8 poison damage. At the end of each
target's turn, they make a constitution save to end the

SEEMING
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: A-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 Feet
Duration: 8 Hours
Components: HS, CM
Cost: 17 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Visual
Description: This genjutsu allows you to change the
appearance of any number of creatures that you can see
within range. You give each target you choose a new,
illusory appearance. An unwilling target can make a
Charisma saving throw, unaffected by this genjutsu on a
successful save.
The genjutsu disguises physical appearances as well as
clothing, armor, weapons, and equipment. You can make
each creature seem 1 foot shorter or taller and appear
thin, fat, or in-between. You can’t change a target’s
body type, so you must choose a form that has the same
basic arrangement of limbs. Otherwise, the extent of the
illusion is up to you. The genjutsu lasts for the duration,
unless you dismiss it sooner. The changes wrought by
this genjutsu fail to hold up to physical inspections. For
example, if you use this genjutsu to add a hat to a
creature’s outfit, objects pass through the hat, and
anyone who touches it would feel nothing or would feel
the creature’s head and hair. If you use this genjutsu to
appear thinner then you are, the hand of someone who
reaches out to touch you would bump into you while it
was seemingly still in midair.
A creature can use its action to inspect a target and
make an Intelligence (Investigation) or Wisdom
(Illusion) check against your genjutsu save DC. If it
succeeds, it becomes aware that the target is disguised.
UNLIMITED PAIN
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: A-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 60 Feet
Duration: Concentration, Up to 1 minute
Components: HS, CM, CS
Cost: 18 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Tactile, Fuinjutsu
Description: You target a creature and maximize their
nervous systems sensitivity to pain.
One creature you can see within range must make an
Intelligence saving throw. On a failed save, for the
duration, the first the target creature takes damage each
turn, they take an additional 3d8 psychic damage.
If this jutsu is cast on a creature who is currently
under the effects of the Doubled Pain is targeted with
this jutsu, they make their saving throw at disadvantage
and if they fail the saving throw, you lose concentration
on the doubled pain genjutsu.
If a creature is targeted by the Unrelenting Pain
genjutsu while under the effects of this genjutsu, they
make their saving throw at disadvantage.

S-RANK:
ANTIPATHY/SYMPATHY
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: S-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Hour
Range: 60 Feet
Duration: 10 Days
Components: HS, CM, CS
Cost: 28 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Visual
Description: This genjutsu attracts or repels creatures of
your choice. You target something within range, either a
huge or smaller object or creature or an area that is no
larger than a 200-foot cube. Then specify a kind of
intelligent creature, such as Sage’s beasts, Demons, or
even Humans. You inject the target with an aura that
either attracts or repels the specified creatures for the
duration. Choose antipathy or sympathy as the aura’s
effect.
Antipathy. The genjutsu causes creatures of the kind
you designated to feel an intense urge to leave the area
and avoid the target. When such a creature can see the
target or comes within 60 feet of it, the creature must
make a Wisdom saving throw or become frightened. The
creature remains frightened while it can see the target or
is within 60 feet of it. While frightened by the target, the
creature must use its movement to move to the nearest
safe spot from which it can’t see the target. If the
creature moves more than 60 feet from the target and
can’t see it, the creature is no longer frightened, but the
creature becomes frightened again if it regains sight of
the target or moves within 60 feet of it.
Sympathy. The enchantment causes the specified
creatures to feel an intense urge to approach the target
while within 60 feet of it or able to see it. When such a
creature can see the target or comes within 60 feet of it,
the creature must make on a Wisdom saving throw or
use its movement on each of its turns to enter the area or
move within reach of the target. When the creature has
done so, it can’t willingly move away from the target. If
the target damages or otherwise harms an affected
creature, the affected creature can make a Wisdom
saving throw to end the effect, as described below.
Ending the Effect. If an affected creature ends its turn
while not within 60 feet of the target or able to see it, the
creature makes a Wisdom saving throw. On a successful
save, the creature is no longer affected by the target and
recognizes the feeling of repugnance or attraction as
magical. In addition, a creature affected by the genjutsu
is allowed another Wisdom saving throw every 24 hours
while the genjutsu persists. A creature that successfully
saves against this effect is immune to it for 1 minute,
after which time it can be affected again.

DIE
Classification: Genjutsu
Rank: S-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 Feet
Duration: Instant
Components: HS, CM
Cost: 30 Chakra
Keywords: Genjutsu, Auditory, Tactile
Description: You utter a word of absolute authority
commanding a creature to attack itself with its most
potent attack. Targeting a creature within range. If the
creature you choose has 100 hit points or fewer, it dies. If
the creature you choose has 101-200 hit points, it must
make a Charisma saving throw, dying on a failed save. If
the creature you choose has 201-300 hits points it must
make a Charisma saving throw, rolling 3d20 taking the
highest result, dying on a failed save. If the creature as
more than 300 Hit Points, the Genjutsu has no effect.
Once a creature has successfully saved against this
Genjutsu, they cannot be effected by it for 24 hours.

Casting Time: 1 Action

RABBIT FURY
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: D-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 Feet
Duration: Instant
Components: M
Cost: 5 Chakra
Keywords: Taijutsu
Description: As a requirement to activate this jutsu, you
must currently be in the Rabbit Fist Stance.
You enter a blitzing stance showing your overwhelming
speed and agility as you bounce between spaces attacking
creatures you pass by with each movement.
Select a space you can see that can hold you, within 30
feet as you quickly dash, leap, and move in as straight a
line as possible moving through all creatures spaces, even
hostile creatures, ending your movement in the select
space. If you move through a large, or larger creatures
space, you count each space of theirs you move through as
if it’s a separate creature for the purpose of this jutsu.
You can target up to 3 creatures whose space you
moved though. Each creature you target must make a
Dexterity saving throw, taking double your unarmed
damage + 1d4 and being knocked prone on a failed or
half as much damage on a successful save.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above D-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the
number of creatures you can target by +1.

COLLIER SHOOT
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action or 1 Reaction, which you take when
you or another creature within 5 feet of you becomes the
target of a ranged Ninjutsu, Taijutsu, or weapon attack
Range: Unarmed Range
Duration: Instant
Components: M
Cost: 7 Chakra
Keywords: Taijutsu, Combo
Description: You leap off the ground and perform a
powerful roundhouse kick targeting a creature’s neck as
you attempt to drag them into the ground. Make a melee
taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal your unarmed damage +
2d8 and the target must make a Strength saving throw. On
a failed save, the target is knocked prone and is embedded
into the ground as you kick them no more than 2 feet into
the ground. A creature embedded into the ground is
counted as Grappled and restrained by the ground itself,
and must make a Strength (Athletics) check vs your
Taijutsu save DC to pull themselves from the ground at the
beginning of each of their turns or as an action on their
turn.
Until the end of your turn, you can target one affected
creature with a Taijutsu Finisher regardless of range, once
per turn, using either an Action or Bonus action, ignoring
its listed casting time.
Alternatively, when you or an allied creature within 5
feet of you would be the target of a ranged Ninjutsu,
Taijutsu or Weapon attack, you can leap into the air and
perform a powerful sweeping kick, in an attempt to strike
the incoming attack, completely shattering or deflecting it.
Make a contested check using your melee taijutsu attack vs
the attacking creatures attack roll. On a success, you are
able to completely divert the attack, knocking it away into
a harmless direction.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu above
C-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the damage
by 1d8. If cast at B-Rank Rank, if used as a Reaction, on a
successful contested check you are able to redirect it
towards another creature within 30 feet of you, using the
result of your check as the attack roll.

ELEMENTANIA
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Unarmed Range
Duration: Instant.
Components: CM, M
Cost: 9 Chakra
Keywords: Taijutsu, Finisher
Description: As a part of casting this jutsu, you can only
target a creature whom you can target with a Finisher as a
result of a Jutsu cast with the Combo keyword.
You bob and weave in and out of a creatures combat
range, building up momentum, preparing yourself for a
massive strike that takes the shape and visage of a roaring
elemental storm.
Make a melee taijutsu attack, dealing your unarmed
damage + 3d12. Your attack gains the following benefits
based on the elemental affinity you wished to imitate (Pick
one when you cast this jutsu)
• Earth: The ground and earth around you shakes and
upheaves. All creatures within 10 feet of the original
target must make a Dexterity saving throw, taking 3d10
bludgeoning damage from the erupting earth.
• Wind: The wind whips and whistles as you forcefully
move it out of your way. Your attack instead deals Wind
damage and the target must make a Constitution saving
throw, gaining 1 rank of the Bleeding condition on a failed
save.
• Fire: Your attack creates such wind resistance that your
striking limb ignites into flame on impact. Your attack
instead deals Fire damage and the target must make a
Constitution saving throw, gaining 1 rank of the burned
condition on a failed save.
• Water: Your attack becomes graceful, like flowing water,
allowing you to strike far more effectively. Your attack
instead deals Cold damage and the target must make a
Constitution saving throw, gaining 1 rank of the Chilled
condition on a failed save.
• Lightning: Your attack is swift, decisive and powerful,
like lightning. Your attack instead deals Lightning
damage and the target must make a Constitution saving
throw, gaining the Shocked condition on a failed save.

If this jutsu is used as a Finisher, it must be against a
falling target. You make three falling, lateral axe kicks
attempting to cripple the target. Make three melee
taijutsu attacks. On a hit, you deal your unarmed damage
+ 2d6. If at least two of these attacks hit, the target is
dazed until the end of their next turn.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the
number of taijutsu attacks by +1.

Duration: Instant
Components: CM, M

attacks of opportunities and if you would end this
movement within 5 feet of a hostile creature you can
perform a powerful sweeping kick by making a single
melee Taijutsu attack, dealing your unarmed damage +
4d6.
• If you choose to kick another creature, make a single
melee taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal your unarmed
damage +4d6, and propel yourself up to 30 feet in any
direction. This movement cannot provoke attacks of
opportunity and if you would end this movement within
5 feet of a hostile creature, you can perform a power
sweeping kick by making another melee taijutsu attack
targeting this creature, dealing your unarmed damage +
4d6.

of times you can propel yourself by striking a creature by +1.

DEATH BLOSSOM
Classification: Taijutsu

Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Unarmed Range
Duration: Instant
Components: M
Cost: 20 Chakra
Keywords: Taijutsu, Finisher

strike. This jutsu can only be used against a creature who
is dazed or prone and who is not a construct.
Select a target creature in range, the target creature
must make a constitution saving throw. On a failed save,
they take your unarmed damage die + 10d6 necrotic
damage which cannot be reduced by any means or half as
much damage on a success.

target instead takes your unarmed damage die + 10d8 in
necrotic damage and suffers 5 ranks of the bleeding
condition, which occurs internally. While bleeding in
this way, these ranks of bleeding are reduced by 1 at the
end of the affected creatures turns. They cannot end the
bleeding condition using a medicine check, instead these
ranks of bleeding can be removed as per usual with a
jutsu with the medical keyword that removes the
bleeding condition being cast at A-Rank or higher.
If a creature has its hit points reduced to 0 as a result
of this jutsu or bleeding cause by this jutsu, their heart
explodes killing them instantly.

or half much damage on a successful save. If you possess
an elemental affinity, you may choose to imbue this
Jutsu with it, if you do so, this jutsu gains the
corresponding Keyword, deals that element’s primary
damage type (water=Cold), and gains additional benefits
based on which elemental affinity you imbue this jutsu
with.
• Earth: All creatures within 5 feet of your target must also
make the saving throw as if they were your primary
target.
• Wind: On a failed saving throw, the target creature gains 5
ranks of the bleeding condition.
• Fire: The damage die size is increased by 1 step.
• Water: On a failed saving throw, the target creature is
knocked back 30 feet in any direction of your choice. If a
creature knocked back by this jutsu collides with a solid
object, their movement is stopped, taking falling damage
as if they had fallen the full distance they were knocked
back.
• Lightning: The range of the Jutsu is increased to 30 feet.
Regardless of whether your target passes or fails their
saving throw, you move to a space within 5 feet of the
original target.

A-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and damage by
2d10.

them. Make a single melee taijutsu attack. On a hit, you

and restrained on a failed check.

action to attempt to escape by making a Strength saving
throw.

speak, or make any Strength of Dexterity ability checks.

affected creature must begin to make constitution saving
throws at beginning of each of their turns. On a failed
save, they fall unconscious.
While you are grappling a creature in this way, your
movement speed is reduced to 0f, you cannot form
handseals, and you cannot concentrate on ninjutsu or
genjutsu.

Description: You leap into the air before crashing down
on a spot within range creating a devastating shockwave
and leaving a mark on the earth.
Each creature within 120 feet must make a Dexterity
saving throw taking 14d10 Bludgeoning damage and
being thrown back a number of feet equal to 5 times your
strength score on a failed save, or half as much damage
on a successful save and no additional effects.
This jutsu deals quadruple damage to structures and
the affected area becomes difficult terrain.

GALACTIC IMPACT

Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Special
Duration: Instant
Components: M

Keyword: Taijutsu

On a successful save, they take half as much damage

Components: CM, M

You raise your hand to the sky, beckoning the heavens

then attempt to create a direct fissure through the prone
target into the underworld with a single blow. When you

of destruction powered by your essence alone.
Make a single melee taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal
10d12 +60 bludgeoning damage.
Regardless of a hit or miss, all creatures, excluding

After using this jutsu, you cannot use it again until you

Components: M

LARIAT
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: S-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Unarmed Range
Duration: Instant
Components: CM, M
Cost: 25 Chakra
Keyword: Taijutsu, Finisher
Description: You rush forward with your arm stretched
out attempting to blow away your target with a clean
strike. Make a melee Taijutsu attack, on a hit you deal
your unarmed damage +10d10 bludgeoning damage and
forces the target creature to make a Constitution saving
throw, being dazed and knocked back 60 feet on a failed
save. If you possess an elemental affinity, you may
choose to imbue this Jutsu with it, if you do so, this jutsu
gains the corresponding Keyword, deals that element’s
primary damage type (water=Cold), and gains additional
benefits based on which elemental affinity you imbue
this jutsu with.
If this jutsu is used as a Finisher, the target creature
makes their saving throw at disadvantage.
• Earth: The target creature gains the slowed condition
until the end of their next turn on a failed saving throw.
• Wind: On a failed saving throw, the target creature gains
8 ranks of the bleeding condition.
• Fire: The damage die size is increased by 1 step.
• Water: On a failed saving throw, the target creature is
knocked back an additional 30 feet. If a creature knocked
back by this jutsu collides with a solid object, their
movement is stopped, taking falling damage as if they
had fallen the full distance they were knocked back.
• Lightning: This jutsu becomes unreactable.

At the conclusion of this attack, all of the blood in the users
body is boiled out of them, forcing the end of their Heavenly
breaths and/or 8 Inner Gates, they then begin to turn to ash,
crumbling into dust over the course of the next minute.

ONE THOUSAND METER PUNCH
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: S-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Unarmed Range
Duration: Instant
Components: M, CM
Cost: 30 Chakra
Keyword: Taijutsu

possess. Make a melee taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal
your unarmed damage +10d10 bludgeoning damage and
force the target creature to make a Constitution saving
throw. On a failed save the target creature is knocked
back 1 mile. If they strike a solid surface that’s less than
5 feet thick, they tear through it, taking 6d6 bludgeoning
damage for each surface they go through. If they strike a
solid surface that’s more than 5 feet thick, their
movement stops and they take 6d6 bludgeoning
damage. it, taking 6d6 bludgeoning damage. If the

SHATTERING STAR: BURST
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: S-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 60 feet
Duration: Instant
Components: M, CM
Cost: (Special) 30 Chakra
Keyword: Taijutsu
Description: Your muscles all over your body tense up
and grow in size as you intend to wreak as much havok
on a creature as possible. Select on creature within
range. You move faster than the eye can see, teleporting
to a space 30 feet above the creature. Make a melee
taijutsu attack, striking the creature directly in the head.
On a hit, you deal your unarmed damage + 3d6.
If you hit with this attack, you then move at
hypersonic speeds once more, striking the creature
with punches and kicks faster than the eye can see.
Make a number of melee taijutsu attacks equal to your
taijutsu ability modifier, dealing your unarmed damage
on each hit. Also on each hit, you knock the creature
15 feet in the direction of your attack, teleporting next to
them to make your next attack, until you make all of
your attacks.
For every attack you land, the target must make a
Strength saving throw, becoming weakened and unable
to take reactions until the end of the turn. With each
additional attack you land after the 2nd, the target
suffers a -1 to this saving throw. If you hit with at least 5
attacks this way, the target automatically fails their
saving throw.
If the creature you attacked failed their saving throw
against this jutsu, as they are disoriented from your

assault, you can target them with one Ninjutsu of C-
Rank or lower, that requires a melee ninjutsu attack and

only effects one creature, at no extra cost. This ninjutsu
automatically hits the creature, and in addition, the
jutsu generates a powerful explosion that knocks the
creature back 30 feet.

SSSTYLISH COMPLIMENTS
Classification: Taijutsu
Rank: S-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Bonus Action
Range: Self
Duration: Special, up to 1 minute
Components: CM, M
Cost: Special (30 Chakra)
Keywords: Taijutsu
Description: You begin to perform a series of extremely
disrespectful movements designed to not only show how
absolutely amazing you are, but how woefully unprepared
your enemies are to deal with someone of your caliber.
While you are under the effects of this jutsu you gain the
following;
• For the next minute, each time you would score two
successful hits on a creature you gain a +1 bonus to your
AC and all saving throws up to a maximum of +4 until the
beginning of your next turn.
• For the duration, you cannot suffer penalties to your AC,
attack rolls or saving throws.
• When you would succeed a saving throw that would
inflict a condition or deal damage, you instead suffer no
effects or damage.
• When a creature would miss you with an attack, you may
take the attack action making an unarmed or weapon
attack targeting the triggering creature.
• The first time you would take damage, or suffer any hostile
creatures effect, this jutsu immediately ends.

Rank: D-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Weapon Range
Duration: Special
Components: W (Melee, Reach, Grapple), M
Cost: 4 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Combo

damage.

make a Strength saving throw. On a successful save, the
creature breaks out of your restraints.

can target them with a Bukijutsu Finisher regardless of
range, using either an Action or Bonus action, ignoring its
listed casting time. If you target the restrained creature with
a Bukijutsu Finisher this Jutsu ends.

Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You begin to throw your weapons rapidly
targeting your enemies pressure point to puncture them.
Make a ranged taijutsu attack against a creature within
range. On a hit, you deal your weapons damage + 3d4 and
force the target creature to make a Constitution saving
throw, gaining the Weakened condition until the end of
their next turn on a failed saving throw.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above D-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and
the number of attacks by +1.

Components: W (Any ranged), M

DRAGON’S END
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: D-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Reaction, which you take when you would
be reduced to 0 Hit Points.
Range: 60 feet
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Any), M
Cost: 5 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You launch your weapon towards an allied
creature you can see in range, ordering them to avenge you
with it.
Select one willing creature you can see in range, and
you throw your chosen weapon to them. Until the end of
the current turn, the weapon thrown retains all bonuses
and effects as if you were wielding it.
The target allied creature can spend their reaction
upon catching your weapon to make cast any one
Bukijutsu you know of equal rank to the rank this jutsu
was cast at, using the weapon you gave them and using
your Taijutsu to hit bonus and Taijutsu save DC.

above D-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3.
ENHANCED BLOCKING

Range: Self
Duration: 1 minute
Components: W (Melee, Blocking), M
Cost: 4 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu

FLYING SWALLOW: SPINNING TOP
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: D-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Self (5-foot radius)
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Any Melee), M
Cost: 5 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: As a part of the activation of this jutsu, you
must have a weapon in both hands that meet this
bukijutsu’s component requirements.
You spin like a top striking all creatures in your
immediate vicinity. All creatures within 5 feet of you
must make a Dexterity saving throw, taking both of your
weapons damage + 2d6 on a failed saving throw or half
as much damage on a successful saving throw.
Depending on the damage type of the chosen weapons
creatures who fail their saving throws suffer additional
effects. If your weapons deal different damage types you
pick one of the following effects.
• Bludgeoning: Affected creatures are knocked prone.
• Piercing: Affected creatures gains 1 rank of Weakened
condition until the end of their next turn.
• Slashing: Affected creatures gain 1 rank of the
Bleeding condition.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above D-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the
damage by 1d6. If this Jutsu is cast at B-Rank or higher,
increase the size of the radius by 5 feet.

Strength saving throw, all chained creatures are freed.

Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: D-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Weapons Range
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Melee Bludgeoning), M
Cost: 5 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Combo
Description: You point your weapon at a creature within
range and begin rapidly extending its range. Make a melee
taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal your weapons damage +
2d6 and force the target to make a Strength saving throw,
being knocked back 10 feet on a failed saving throw. If this
movement is stopped by a structure or object, they take
falling damage as if they fell the full distance they were
knocked back.
The knock back of this jutsu is increased if your weapon
has the reach property. For each stage of reach your weapon
has the knock back is increased by 20 feet.

creature with a Bukijutsu Finisher regardless of range,
once per turn, using either an Action or Bonus action,
ignoring its listed casting time.

above D-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3. If this
Jutsu is cast at B-Rank or higher, the knock back is
increased by 30 feet for each stage of reach your weapon
has. If this Jutsu is cast at S-Rank or higher, the knock
back is increased by 40 feet for each stage of reach your
weapon has.

Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: D-Rank

Range: Weapon Range
Duration: 1 Round
Components: W (Any Bow)
Cost: 4 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You quickly prepare multiple shots from

ranged taijutsu attack if a creature triggers attacks of

beginning of their next turn.

Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You fire off every receptor in your mind and

For the duration, select one creature to soul mark. The
selected creature cannot benefit from three fourths, half
or partial cover. A creature remains marked until it
reaches 0 hit points. You can only have 1 creature soul
marked at any given time.

Range: 10ft Cube
Duration: Instant
Components: NT (Trappers Kit)
Cost: 3 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu

CHAIN SLAM
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Weapon Range
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Melee, Reach, Grapple), M
Cost: 8 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Finisher
Description: As a part of casting this jutsu you must have
someone restrained by your weapon. You tug on your
weapon with enough force to throw the restrained creature
into the air, before slamming them into the ground in
another space within your weapons range, forcing the
restrained creature to make a Constitution saving throw. On
a failed saving throw the target creature fails to brace
themselves, taking 5d10 bludgeoning damage. On a
successful save, the target creature takes half as much
damage. Regardless of if the target creature passes or fails
their saving throw, they are no longer restrained and are
forced prone.
If this jutsu is used as a Finisher, you instead deal your
6d10 bludgeoning damage and creatures gain the dazed
condition on a failed saving throw.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu above
C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the damage
by 1d10.

FLOWER ART: FALSE BOUQUET
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 10 Minutes
Range: Special
Duration: 8 hours
Components: W (Flower), M, CM
Cost: 8 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijustu
Description: You prepare a bouquet of flowers that have
been primed to explode at your command. Creatures that
see this bouquet must make a Wisdom saving throw against
your Bukijutsu DC, believe that the bouquet is harmless on a
failed saving throw and discover that the bouquet is
dangerous on a successful saving throw.
While the bouquet is in your line of sight or within 300
feet of you, you may detonate it as either a bonus action
or reaction. When you do so, you force all creatures
within 10 feet of it to make a Dexterity saving throw.
Creatures who believe the bouquet to be harmless make
this saving throw at disadvantage. On a failed saving
throw creatures take 7d8 fire damage. On a successful
saving throw creatures take half as much damage.
If you have not detonated the bouquet within 8 hours,
your chakra disappears from the flowers turning it back
into a normal bouquet.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu above
C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the damage
by 2d8. If this Jutsu is cast at B-Rank, the duration increases
to 1 Day. If this Jutsu is cast at S-Rank, the duration
increases to 1 Week.

Rank: C-Rank

Duration: Concentration, up to 10 minutes
Components: W (Flower), M, CM

Keywords: Bukijustu, Medical
Description: You fuel a flower with your chakra, causing it

The transformation of the ground is camouflaged to look
natural. Any creature that can't see the area at the time the
Jutsu is cast must make a Wisdom (Perception) check
against your Taijutsu save DC to recognize the terrain as
hazardous before entering it.

C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3. If this Jutsu is
cast at A-Rank, the Jutsu no longer requires concentration.

KILLER BEE
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Bonus Action
Range: Self
Duration: Concentration, up to 1 minute.
Components: W (x5 Melee, Slashing), M
Cost: 8 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You can not lose concentration on this jutsu
as a result of damage. You hold 5 of your weapons in the
gaps between your armpits, the hinge joints of your arms
and legs, and various other places; before placing a final
weapon in your mouth, leaving your hands completely free.
This jutsu allows for quick and unpredictable attacks, that
catch your enemies off-guard. While under the effects of
this jutsu, you cannot concentrate on any other jutsu.
This jutsu provides you the following benefits;
• The first melee Taijutsu or weapon attack you make
per turn is unreactable.
• As a reaction you can deflect an attack against you,
adding 1d6 to your AC against the triggering attack. If
this would cause the attack to miss, you can make a
melee weapon attack as a part of the same reaction, as
long as the target is within range.
• As a bonus action, you can make a single melee
weapon attack.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu above
C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3. If this Jutsu is
cast at A-Rank or higher, you gain 2 additional Reactions
per turn that you can only use to activate the unique
reaction of this jutsu.

Jutsu.
If you summon slashing weapons, you make a single
ranged Taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal 5d10 slashing
damage and apply a rank of the bleeding condition.
If you summon piercing weapons, you make a single
ranged Taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal 5d10 piercing
damage and apply a rank of the bleeding condition.
If you summon bludgeoning weapons, you make a
single ranged taijutsu attack. On a hit, you deal 5d12
bludgeoning damage.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3. If this
jutsu is cast at A-Rank, you make 2 attacks against the
same creature using two different damage types. If this
jutsu is cast at S-Rank or higher, you make 3 attacks
against the same creature using every damage type.
MANIPULATED TOOLS: HEAVY BALLS
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: 30 feet
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Weapon Scroll), M
Cost: 7 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Fuinjutsu
Description: You throw your weapon scroll above a
creature within range, before activating the scroll to
summon a massive, weighted ball. The target creature
must make a Strength saving throw, taking 4d10
bludgeoning damage and being restrained and forced
prone by the weighted ball on a failed saving throw.
Creatures restrained by this Jutsu may make a
Strength saving throw at the beginning of each of their
turns, or as an Action on their turn to escape from the
weighted ball.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above D-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3,
damage by 1d10, and the range by 15 feet. If this Jutsu is
cast at A-Rank or higher, you may target 1 additional
creature within range.

MANIPULATED TOOLS: METAL HURRICANE
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Self (30-foot line)
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Weapon Scroll), M
Cost: 7 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Fuinjutsu
Description: You unravel your weapon scroll as you
begin to summon an armories worth of weapons that are
sent flying forwards in a 30-foot line from you. You
choose what type of weapon you summon, changing the
effects of the Jutsu.
If you summon slashing weapons, all creatures within
the line must make a Dexterity saving throw, taking 5d8
slashing damage and gaining 2 ranks of the bleeding
condition on a failed saving throw or half as much
damage on a successful saving throw.
If you summon bludgeoning weapons, all creatures
within the line must make a Strength saving throw,
taking 5d8 bludgeoning damage and gaining the dazed
condition on a failed saving throw or half as much
damage on a successful saving throw.
If you summon piercing weapons, all creatures within
the line must make a Constitution saving throw, taking
5d8 piercing damage and gaining 2 ranks of the bleeding
condition on a failed saving throw or half as much
damage on a successful saving throw.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu
above C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3. If you
cast this Jutsu at S-Rank or higher, you summon
weapons of all types forcing all creatures in the line to
make all three saving throws.
MIKIRI COUNTER
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Reaction, being hit with a melee attack.
Range: Self
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Any Melee Weapon), M
Cost: 9 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You see a melee attack coming at you and
your body doesn’t allow it to proceed any further. You
instinctively use your hand or feet to not only attempt to
parry the attack, but to also drive the attack into the
ground, granting you the chance to counterattack.
When you are hit with a melee attack, make a melee
Taijutsu attack, if your attack result is higher than the
triggering attack’s result, the attack misses and you may
make a single weapon attack or cast a Bukijutsu. The
Bukijutsu must be C-Rank or lower, require an attack
roll, and only target the triggering creature.

MIKIRI SWING
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: C-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Reaction, which you take when you would
be hit with a ranged weapon attack
Range: Self
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Any Melee), M
Cost: 6 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu
Description: You see a ranged attack coming at you and
reflexively parry it, driving the attack into the ground.
When you are hit with a ranged weapon attack, make a
melee weapon attack roll. If the result of this roll is greater
than the triggering attack roll, the attack instead misses.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast above C-rank,
increase the cost by 3. If this Jutsu is cast at A-Rank or
Higher, you may now cast this Jutsu in response to any
ranged attack instead of any ranged weapon attack.

Classification: Bukijutsu

Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Weapons Range
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Melee Bludgeoning), M

Keywords: Bukijutsu, Finisher

distracting twirls designed to keep them away.

Constitution saving throw, gaining the slowed condition on

inside your target draining their strength and slowing their
movements.
The affected creature may make a Strength or
Constitution saving throw at the beginning of each of
their turns, or as an Action on their turn to remove the
roots from themselves and remove the slowed condition
from themselves.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu above
C-Rank, increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and the damage
by 1d6.

Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: B-Rank
Casting Time: 1 Action
Range: Weapons Range
Duration: Instant
Components: W (Melee Bludgeoning), M
Cost: 13 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Finisher
Description: You twirl your weapon around yourself,

range. Make a melee Taijustu attack. On a hit, you deal your

Strength saving throw as you begin to extend the length of
your weapon. On a failed saving throw, the target is lifted 30

feet away from you, taking fall damage as if they had fallen
an equal distance.

your weapon has the reach property. For each stage of reach
your weapon has, you increase the distance you lift the
creature by 20 feet.
If this jutsu is used as a Finisher, instead of taking fall
damage the creature takes 1d8 bludgeoning damage for
every 10 feet they were lifted into the air.
At Higher Ranks: For each rank you cast this jutsu above
B-Rank increase the cost of this jutsu by 3 and distance a
creature is lifted into the air by 20 feet.

as much damage as the initial target on a failed save.

Constitution saving throw.
• Lightning: The Jutsu becomes unreactable.

creature with a Bukijutsu Finisher regardless of range, once
per turn, using either an Action or Bonus action, ignoring its
listed casting time.

damage by 1d10.

Rank: S-Rank

Range: Weapons Range
Duration: Instant
Components: M, W (Any Bow)
Cost: 30 Chakra
Keywords: Bukijutsu, Finisher
Description: You fire a single shot from your bow, aiming

target creature must make a Constitution saving throw,
taking 15d12 bludgeoning damage and having one of their
limbs disabled for 1d4 days on a failed saving throw or half
as much damage on a successful saving throw. If the target
fails their saving throw, you choose which limb is disabled.
If you disable one of your opponent’s arms, they lose the
ability to cast Jutsu with the HS component. If you disable
one of your opponents’ legs, they gain 1 rank of the Slowed
condition. A creature can have their disabled limb healed if
they are targeted with a A-Rank or higher medical jutsu.
If this jutsu is used as a Finisher, the targets limbs are
disabled for 3d4 days instead.

END TIMES
Classification: Bukijutsu
Rank: S-Rank

Range: 120 Foot Cone

Components: W (Any), CM, M

Description: You turn your weapon into a herald of

of you. With a single movement of your weapon, you
begin your destruction. All creatures in a 120 foot cone in
front of you must make a Constitution saving throw,
taking your weapons damage + 25d6 damage on a failed
saving throw or half as much on a successful saving
throw. If you possess an elemental affinity, you may
choose to imbue this Jutsu with it, if you do so, this jutsu
gains the corresponding Keyword, deals that element’s
primary damage type (water=Cold), and gains additional
benefits based on which elemental affinity you imbue
this jutsu with.
• Earth: The affected area is counted as difficult terrain and
creatures that are more than 10 feet from each other are
heavily obscured from one another.
• Wind: On a failed saving throw, creatures gain 5 ranks of
the bleeding condition. Additionally, flying creatures
within the area of your Jutsu make their saving throw at
disadvantage.
• Fire: All items not being worn or carried in the area of
your Jutsu are ignited, being permanently destroyed.
Additionally, on a failed saving throw, creatures gain 2
ranks of the Burning condition.
• Water: On a failed saving throw, creatures are knocked
back to the edge of your cone. If a creature knocked back
by this jutsu collides with a solid object, their movement
is stopped, taking falling damage as if they had fallen the
full distance they were knocked back.
• Lightning: This jutsu becomes unreactable.
